#### [MEDICAL CENTRES](#medical-centres)

# ACCREDITED MEDICAL CENTRES

The following is a list of Medical Centers in all Malaysian labour Source Countries accredited to undertake Bio-Medical health examination of prospective workers bound for Malaysia.\
Please note that the Bio-Medical process is mandatory before being allowed to proceed with eVDR applications.

The list below is regularly updated as new Medical Centers are constantly being added to the list. Please check back for upadates.

*Note: Accredited Medical Centers are registered with their respective local Government authorities and have undergone the necessary audit procedures by Bestinet Sdn. Bhd.*
